<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">          else</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> start </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Math.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">max</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(end </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> step, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">          if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (end </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x3C;=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> start) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> r</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">        return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">r, start: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">toValue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(start), end: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">toValue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(end) }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      }),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    }))</span></span>
